11	# 审稿报告：Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal
12	
13	**论文**: Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld
14	**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (via ACL Rolling Review) — Interpretability and Analysis track
15	**审稿模式**: Fresh review (Round 26)
16	**Reviewer 配置**: R1 (方法严谨性+实验设计, 3.5 Borderline Conference) / R2 (新颖性+完整性+相关工作, 3.0 Findings)
17	**交叉分享**: 1 轮交叉，关键问题达成高度共识（8 个 AGREE / 2 个 REBUT / 1 个 partial AGREE）
18	
19	---
20	
21	## Summary
22	
23	论文提出 episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework，通过纯策略比较分解 LLM agent routing 信号的构成。在 ScienceWorld（11 task types, 245 episodes）上发现 78.9% 的非 tie routing labels 源于 death asymmetry（内部策略因幻觉触发致命失败，确定性策略存活）。DAF（Death Avoidance Factor）在三个 7-9B 模型家族上达到 88-90%。跨环境 probes（MATH 无信号, ALFWorld 为 information asymmetry）展示了框架的诊断能力但同时表明 death dominance 是环境特定的。Router 训练产出 negative result（step-level balanced accuracy 56-59%）。
24	
25	---
26	
27	## Strengths
28	
29	**S1. 实验设计的系统性和统计严谨性** [AGREE x2]
30	跨三个模型家族（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B）的一致性验证、跨三个环境的 probes、多项 ablation（text augmentation, grounding, capacity scaling, Reflexion, retry）。统计处理在 NLP 论文中属上佳水平：episode-level bootstrap (B=10,000)、cluster bootstrap、wild cluster bootstrap、leave-one-task-out sensitivity、Bonferroni correction、penalty sensitivity analysis。
31	
32	**S2. 诚实的负面结果呈现** [AGREE x2]
33	Router training 低 accuracy（§3.6）、cross-environment probes 否定了 death dominance 的泛化性（§3.4）、text augmentation 无提升（Appendix J）均如实报告。论文在 MATH/ALFWorld 上的 "boundary probes" 定位是诚实的。
34	
35	**S3. 写作质量** [AGREE x2]
36	8 页正文 + 20 个 Appendices（A-T）的安排合理。核心 claim 表述清晰，实验 narrative 流畅。Limitations 章节覆盖面好（5 个子段：环境范围、方法论约束、统计限制、pure-strategy upper bound、诊断范围）。
37	
38	**S4. 可复现性** [AGREE x2]
39	Algorithm 1 伪代码、prompt templates（Appendix T）、router 训练配置（Appendix I）、per-task 分解（Appendix C）均有详细说明。Reproducibility 4/5。
40	
41	**S5. 框架设计完整性** [R1 强调]
42	Episode-level labeling + DAF decomposition + SCI 三指标形成完整诊断工具链。Algorithm 1 的 edge case 处理（both-dead, both-alive-tied, score-comparison）thorough。Label quality audit（Appendix B）增加数据透明度。
43	
44	---
45	
46	## Weaknesses
47	
48	### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）
49	
50	**N1. DAF "dominance" 由 penalty 幅度驱动，正文未充分反映 penalty sensitivity** [AGREE x2, critical]
51	- Table 12 (Appendix K): P=0 时 DAF=7.4%, P=10 时 DAF=39.8%, P=100 时 DAF=90.1%。DAF=90% 是 reward design 的产物（P=100 在 0-100 score range 内为极端惩罚），而非 routing 信号的内在属性。
52	- 78.9% label fraction（不受 penalty 加权影响）是更 robust 的指标，但论文在 Abstract/Introduction 仍以 DAF 88-90% 为 prominent metric。
53	- **需要**：(1) 正文 §3.1 或 §5 增加一段正面讨论 Table 12 含义；(2) 以 label fraction 为 primary claim，DAF 降级为补充。
54	
55	**N2. 跨模型验证仅覆盖 7-9B 同级模型** [AGREE x2, critical]
56	- 三个模型参数量和内部死亡率高度相似（~42%），一致性更可能反映 7-9B tier 的共有特征而非跨规模 robustness。
57	- §3.3 报告 72B DAF=92.4%（死亡率 30.6% vs det 2.75%），但位于 Agent Improvement 段而非 Cross-Model 段，且未与 7-9B 结果并列讨论。
58	- **需要**：(1) 将 "cross-model family" 限定为 "within 7-9B tier"；(2) 72B 结果明确定位为 scaling analysis。
59	
60	**N3. "Contradicting the routing literature's implicit assumption" 是 strawman framing** [AGREE x2, critical/strong warning]
61	- 被引 routing 工作（CogRouter, ARES, MTRouter, RouteLLM, Budget-Aware Routing）操作在 model selection、computational routing、multi-turn routing 等无 catastrophic failure 的 settings。
62	- ScienceWorld 的极端 catastrophic failure 使这里发现 death dominance 不构成对这些工作的 "contradiction"——论文自己的 ALFWorld 结果恰好支持了其他 settings 可能有多样化信号。
63	- R1 认为严重程度介于 Must-Fix 和 Should-Fix——本质是 wording 问题，可通过修改几句话解决。
64	- **需要**：将 "contradicting" 改为 "revealing that in catastrophic-failure environments, signal diversity may collapse to a single dominant factor"。
65	
66	### Should-Fix（影响评分但不一票否决）
67	
68	**W1. 框架方法论新颖性** [DIVERGENCE: R2 Must-Fix / R1 Should-Fix]
69	- R2：Algorithm 1 是 if-else 分类树，DAF 数学上平凡，未连接因果推断文献（Pearl/Rubin）。对 Interpretability and Analysis track，期望更形式化的工具。
70	- R1 反驳：Analysis track 的贡献标准是诊断性洞察而非数学框架新颖性。简单方法得出深刻洞察在 probing 文献中常见。DAF 简洁不等于平凡——它将模糊直觉量化为可比较指标。但同意在 Related Work 中应简要讨论与因果推断的关系。
71	- **建议**：Related Work 增加一段讨论与 potential outcomes (Rubin 1974) 的关系和区别。
72	
73	**W2. Router negative result 解释不完整** [AGREE x2]
74	- 仅覆盖 single architecture family (Qwen3) + single training paradigm (LoRA) + 2,040 training steps。
75	- 未排除替代假设：模型容量不足、数据量不够、更好的训练方法（contrastive learning, different loss functions）可能成功。
76	- Oracle margin 仅 2.89 points，进一步降低 negative result 的诊断意义。
77	- **建议**：明确标注为 "preliminary negative result"；补充讨论替代训练方法。
78	
79	**W3. ~50-episode pilot 缺乏 power analysis** [AGREE x2]
80	- 当前推理仅为 "ScienceWorld 效应极大（d=3.02）所以小样本可检测"，但新环境可能有更小效应。
81	- **建议**：补充 power analysis，或将 "suffices" 弱化为 "provides preliminary signal"。
82	
83	**W4. Cluster bootstrap K=11 正文 caveat 不够** [AGREE x2]
84	- K=11 远低于 Cameron et al. (2008) 推荐的 K≥20-30。正文 §3.1 仅简短提及。
85	- **建议**：紧跟一句明确 caveat，引导读者主要依赖非聚类 bootstrap CI。
86	
87	**W5. 11/30 task types 排除 (63%)** [AGREE x2]
88	- 7 个 agent-floor tasks（两策略均近零进展）可能代表 death dominance 不成立的 regime，但 Discussion 未讨论此含义。
89	- Appendix C/S 的排除验证部分缓解但不消除 selection bias 担忧。
90	- **建议**：Discussion 中补充讨论 agent-floor tasks 对 death dominance 的含义。
91	
92	**W6. 跨环境 probes 深度不足** [AGREE x2]
93	- MATH 仅 aggregate-level 无 per-category 分解；ALFWorld 仅一段简短讨论。
94	- ALFWorld DAF=0% 实际上暗示不同环境由不同机制驱动——论文 reframe 为 "环境诊断框架揭示异质机制" 会比 "death avoidance 是主导" 更准确。
95	- **建议**：调整 Abstract/Introduction 避免暗示 generalizability。
96	
97	**W7. 相关工作遗漏** [部分 AGREE]
98	- 因果推断基础（Pearl/Rubin potential outcomes）[AGREE x2]
99	- Agent safety catastrophic failure avoidance 方向 [R2 建议, R1 部分 AGREE]
100	- Credit assignment for LLM agents [R1 REBUT: SALT/CSO 已引用，覆盖面足够]
101	
102	---
103	
104	## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]
105	
106	1. **DAF vs label fraction 的主次定位**：label fraction 78.9% 是 penalty-invariant 的真正 empirical finding，DAF 88-90% 是 penalty-amplified 的补充指标。论文应调整 framing 权重。
107	2. **Framing overclaiming**：Abstract/Introduction 中的 generality claims 超出 ScienceWorld 单一环境的证据支撑。"Contradicting" routing literature 是 strawman。
108	3. **Cross-model 声明范围**：三个 7-9B 模型的一致性不足以声称 "cross-model family" 复制。
109	4. **Router negative result 定位**：应为 "preliminary" 而非 definitive non-learnability。
110	5. **统计 caveats**：K=11 cluster bootstrap、~50-episode pilot 均需更显著的 caveat。
111	6. **核心 novelty/excitement 限制**：death dominance 的方向在 oracle vs. hallucinating-LLM 设置下可预期。§5 Non-triviality 论证（72B persistence, Reflexion DAF>100%, inverted-U）关于 persistence 而非 existence。这是 Findings 而非 Conference 水平的核心原因，不可仅通过 writing 修复。
112	
113	## Reviewer Divergence [REBUT]
114	
115	1. **R2-N2（框架方法论新颖性是否为 Must-Fix）**：R2 认为 Algorithm 1 + DAF 缺乏技术深度，应连接 Pearl/Rubin 形式化框架。R1 反驳——Analysis track 贡献标准不要求数学框架新颖性，简单方法产出深刻洞察是常见且被接受的模式。R1 同意作为 Should-Fix（在 Related Work 中讨论因果推断关系）。
116	   - **Director 判断**：降为 Should-Fix。R1 对 track-specific 贡献标准的论证成立。但论文确实应补充与因果推断文献的连接。
117	
118	2. **R2-W3（DAF 在 ScienceWorld 外 DAF=0 → metric 无独立价值）**：R1 反驳——DAF=0 在无死亡环境中是正确诊断结果，非指标缺陷。DAF 在其他 catastrophic-failure 环境（自动驾驶仿真、safety-critical RL）可非零。但 R2 的核心关切——论文未论证 DAF 在更广泛场景的价值——仍成立。
119	
120	3. **R1-W5（分解公式近似误差）**：R2 澄清 episode-level 是 exact partitioning，但 penalty contribution 层面确有近似（忽略死亡前 partial scores）。论文 §2.3 报告残差 0.23（0.8% of Δ_full），近似质量在 P=100 时良好。降为 minor。
121	
122	---
123	
124	## Questions
125	
126	1. §3.3 报告 72B DAF=92.4%——这比 7B 的 90.4% 还高。如果更大模型降低了死亡率但 DAF 反而上升，这是否意味着 death dominance 在更大模型上更极端？请讨论 72B 的 Δ_nd 值及其含义。
127	2. 61.2% 的 episodes 是 ties（两策略无差异）。这意味着 routing 最多改善 38.8% 的 episodes。论文是否应在 Discussion 中更显式地讨论 routing 的实际价值上限？
128	3. Algorithm 1 对 death-asymmetric episodes 直接赋予 preference label 而不比较 score。在 P < max_possible_partial_score 时此假设是否成立？
129	4. 论文 "counterfactual" 的使用是 potential outcomes 意义上的（两种 treatment 下的 outcome 比较），还是 Pearl 意义上的结构反事实？请在 Related Work 中明确。
130	
131	---
132	
133	## Missing References
134	
135	- **Pearl (2009)** *Causality: Models, Reasoning, and Inference* — 论文大量使用 "counterfactual" 但未引用因果推断基础文献 [R1 R2 均建议]
136	- **Rubin (1974)** potential outcomes framework — 论文的纯策略比较本质上是 potential outcomes [R1 R2 均建议]
137	- **"Check Yourself Before You Wreck Yourself"** (arXiv 2510.16492) — LLM agent catastrophic failure avoidance [R2 建议]
138	- **Credit assignment for RL-based LLM agents** (arXiv 2604.09459) [R2 建议, R1 认为 SALT/CSO 已覆盖]

162	## Overall Assessment
163	
164	**Overall Score: 3.0 (Findings)**
165	**Confidence: 4/5**
166	
167	论文在 ScienceWorld 环境内的实证分析扎实、统计处理严谨、负面结果如实呈现。主要问题在于：
168	
169	1. **Novelty/Excitement 不足**（两位 Reviewer Excitement 2.5-3.0）：核心发现（oracle 策略比 hallucinating-LLM 策略更安全）的方向在 ScienceWorld 设置下接近可预期，§5 Non-triviality 论证关于 persistence 而非 existence。这是 Findings 而非 Conference 水平的核心原因，不可仅通过 writing 修复——需要额外环境的深度分析或根本性新发现。
170	
171	2. **Framing overclaiming**：标题/Abstract 的 generality claims 超出单一环境的证据范围。"Contradicting routing literature" 是 strawman。DAF 88-90% 被 penalty 幅度驱动（P=0 时仅 7.4%）。这些通过 reframing 可部分缓解。
172	
173	3. **Scope 限制**：单一环境（ScienceWorld, 11/30 task types）、同级模型（7-9B）、跨环境 probes 反而证明了 death dominance 不泛化。
174	
175	**如果作者能够**：(a) 将 framing 从 "general routing finding" 调整为 "diagnostic framework + environment-specific case study"；(b) 正文正面讨论 penalty sensitivity；(c) 限定 cross-model 声明范围；(d) 补充因果推断文献连接——论文是一篇合格的 Findings 论文。达到 Conference 水准需要在至少一个额外 catastrophic-failure 环境上复现 death dominance。

186	
187	## Detailed Comments
188	
189	### §1 Introduction
190	- "The shared assumption—that routing signal stems from diverse sources and a learned router can exploit it—remains untested" → 这是 strawman。被引工作在不同 settings 中确实有多样化信号。改为 "whether this assumption holds in environments with catastrophic failure modes remains untested"。
191	- Contribution (1) "episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework" 的 generality claim 需限定。
192	- Contribution (2) 中的 router diagnostic 部分是最有实践价值的发现之一 [R1 R2 共识]，建议提升其 prominence。
193	
194	### §2 Method
195	- §2.3 DAF 定义后应立即注明 DAF 对 P 单调递增，是数学性质而非经验发现。当前措辞 "DAF increases monotonically with P, a mathematical consequence" 存在但不够显著。
196	- 缺少与 potential outcomes framework 的连接（论文的纯策略比较本质上是 treatment effect 估计）。
197	
198	### §3 Experiments
199	- §3.1: "150 (61.2%) are ties" 后的表述可能产生歧义。建议改为 "150 (61.2%) episodes show no preference; routing is relevant in the remaining 95 (38.8%)"。
200	- §3.2: "death dominance replicates across model families" 应限定为 "within the 7-9B tier"。三个模型 ~42% 内部死亡率的一致性更可能反映 tier 共性。
201	- §3.3: 72B DAF=92.4% 是重要数据点但仅在 Agent Improvement 段出现。建议在 §3.2 讨论或至少交叉引用。
202	- §3.4: MATH/ALFWorld probes 深度不足。MATH 缺少 per-category 分解。ALFWorld 的 information asymmetry finding 值得更多讨论——不同环境有不同 dominant mechanism 可能是比 death dominance 更有趣的框架级 contribution。
203	- §3.6: "A Negative Result" 标题低估了此发现的价值。Router 无法学习 task-type-level signal 有独立的实践意义 [R1 R2 共识]。应标注为 "preliminary"。
204	
205	### §4 Related Work
206	- Safe RL 段需扩展：讨论 death avoidance 与 CMDP constraint satisfaction 的对应、为什么 Safe RL 方案不直接适用于 LLM agent routing。
207	- 缺少因果推断文献的讨论（Pearl/Rubin）。
208	- 考虑补充 agent safety catastrophic failure avoidance 方向文献。
209	
210	### §5 Discussion
211	- "Non-triviality of death dominance" 段有效但论证的是 persistence/prevalence 而非 existence 的 surprise。
212	- 建议增加一段讨论 penalty sensitivity 的含义（引用 Table 12）。
213	- 建议讨论 agent-floor tasks 对 death dominance 不成立的 regime 意味着什么。
214	
215	### §6 Conclusion
216	- 视觉审查确认正文 through Conclusion 在 page 8 结束——但 R1 报告末尾可能有 ~2 行溢出，需核查。
217	
218	### Appendices
219	- 20 个 Appendices (A-T) 质量高，覆盖面广。特别是 Appendix R（Decision Framework）的 3 步流程有实践价值。
220	- Algorithm 1 应注明 death-priority 规则的隐含假设（P > max possible partial score）。
221	
222	### 引用
223	- 5 条 not_found + 6 条 mismatch 多为 OpenAlex 索引问题。webb-2023 年份在 R25 已核实正确（CJE vol.56 no.3）。建议再次核查以确保一致。
```

</details>

      "content": "---\nround: 26\nreview_mode: fresh\noverall_score: 3.0 (Findings)\ntimestamp: 2026-05-21T22:28:26.200066+00:00\nreviewer_ids: [1, 2]\ncross_response_reviewer_ids: [1, 2]\nforce_resubmit: False\n---\n\n# 审稿报告：Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal\n\n**论文**: Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (via ACL Rolling Review) — Interpretability and Analysis track\n**审稿模式**: Fresh review (Round 26)\n**Reviewer 配置**: R1 (方法严谨性+实验设计, 3.5 Borderline Conference) / R2 (新颖性+完整性+相关工作, 3.0 Findings)\n**交叉分享**: 1 轮交叉，关键问题达成高度共识（8 个 AGREE / 2 个 REBUT / 1 个 partial AGREE）\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n论文提出 episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework，通过纯策略比较分解 LLM agent routing 信号的构成。在 ScienceWorld（11 task types, 245 episodes）上发现 78.9% 的非 tie routing labels 源于 death asymmetry（内部策略因幻觉触发致命失败，确定性策略存活）。DAF（Death Avoidance Factor）在三个 7-9B 模型家族上达到 88-90%。跨环境 probes（MATH 无信号, ALFWorld 为 information asymmetry）展示了框架的诊断能力但同时表明 death dominance 是环境特定的。Router 训练产出 negative result（step-level balanced accuracy 56-59%）。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1. 实验设计的系统性和统计严谨性** [AGREE x2]\n跨三个模型家族（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B）的一致性验证、跨三个环境的 probes、多项 ablation（text augmentation, grounding, capacity scaling, Reflexion, retry）。统计处理在 NLP 论文中属上佳水平：episode-level bootstrap (B=10,000)、cluster bootstrap、wild cluster bootstrap、leave-one-task-out sensitivity、Bonferroni correction、penalty sensitivity analysis。\n\n**S2. 诚实的负面结果呈现** [AGREE x2]\nRouter training 低 accuracy（§3.6）、cross-environment probes 否定了 death dominance 的泛化性（§3.4）、text augmentation 无提升（Appendix J）均如实报告。论文在 MATH/ALFWorld 上的 \"boundary probes\" 定位是诚实的。\n\n**S3. 写作质量** [AGREE x2]\n8 页正文 + 20 个 Appendices（A-T）的安排合理。核心 claim 表述清晰，实验 narrative 流畅。Limitations 章节覆盖面好（5 个子段：环境范围、方法论约束、统计限制、pure-strategy upper bound、诊断范围）。\n\n**S4. 可复现性** [AGREE x2]\nAlgorithm 1 伪代码、prompt templates（Appendix T）、router 训练配置（Appendix I）、per-task 分解（Appendix C）均有详细说明。Reproducibility 4/5。\n\n**S5. 框架设计完整性** [R1 强调]\nEpisode-level labeling + DAF decomposition + SCI 三指标形成完整诊断工具链。Algorithm 1 的 edge case 处理（both-dead, both-alive-tied, score-comparison）thorough。Label quality audit（Appendix B）增加数据透明度。\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1. DAF \"dominance\" 由 penalty 幅度驱动，正文未充分反映 penalty sensitivity** [AGREE x2, critical]\n- Table 12 (Appendix K): P=0 时 DAF=7.4%, P=10 时 DAF=39.8%, P=100 时 DAF=90.1%。DAF=90% 是 reward design 的产物（P=100 在 0-100 score range 内为极端惩罚），而非 routing 信号的内在属性。\n- 78.9% label fraction（不受 penalty 加权影响）是更 robust 的指标，但论文在 Abstract/Introduction 仍以 DAF 88-90% 为 prominent metric。\n- **需要**：(1) 正文 §3.1 或 §5 增加一段正面讨论 Table 12 含义；(2) 以 label fraction 为 primary claim，DAF 降级为补充。\n\n**N2. 跨模型验证仅覆盖 7-9B 同级模型** [AGREE x2, critical]\n- 三个模型参数量和内部死亡率高度相似（~42%），一致性更可能反映 7-9B tier 的共有特征而非跨规模 robustness。\n- §3.3 报告 72B DAF=92.4%（死亡率 30.6% vs det 2.75%），但位于 Agent Improvement 段而非 Cross-Model 段，且未与 7-9B 结果并列讨论。\n- **需要**：(1) 将 \"cross-model family\" 限定为 \"within 7-9B tier\"；(2) 72B 结果明确定位为 scaling analysis。\n\n**N3. \"Contradicting the routing literature's implicit assumption\" 是 strawman framing** [AGREE x2, critical/strong warning]\n- 被引 routing 工作（CogRouter, ARES, MTRouter, RouteLLM, Budget-Aware Routing）操作在 model selection、computational routing、multi-turn routing 等无 catastrophic failure 的 settings。\n- ScienceWorld 的极端 catastrophic failure 使这里发现 death dominance 不构成对这些工作的 \"contradiction\"——论文自己的 ALFWorld 结果恰好支持了其他 settings 可能有多样化信号。\n- R1 认为严重程度介于 Must-Fix 和 Should-Fix——本质是 wording 问题，可通过修改几句话解决。\n- **需要**：将 \"contradicting\" 改为 \"revealing that in catastrophic-failure environments, signal diversity may collapse to a single dominant factor\"。\n\n### Should-Fix（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1. 框架方法论新颖性** [DIVERGENCE: R2 Must-Fix / R1 Should-Fix]\n- R2：Algorithm 1 是 if-else 分类树，DAF 数学上平凡，未连接因果推断文献（Pearl/Rubin）。对 Interpretability and Analysis track，期望更形式化的工具。\n- R1 反驳：Analysis track 的贡献标准是诊断性洞察而非数学框架新颖性。简单方法得出深刻洞察在 probing 文献中常见。DAF 简洁不等于平凡——它将模糊直觉量化为可比较指标。但同意在 Related Work 中应简要讨论与因果推断的关系。\n- **建议**：Related Work 增加一段讨论与 potential outcomes (Rubin 1974) 的关系和区别。\n\n**W2. Router negative result 解释不完整** [AGREE x2]\n- 仅覆盖 single architecture family (Qwen3) + single training paradigm (LoRA) + 2,040 training steps。\n- 未排除替代假设：模型容量不足、数据量不够、更好的训练方法（contrastive learning, different loss functions）可能成功。\n- Oracle margin 仅 2.89 points，进一步降低 negative result 的诊断意义。\n- **建议**：明确标注为 \"preliminary negative result\"；补充讨论替代训练方法。\n\n**W3. ~50-episode pilot 缺乏 power analysis** [AGREE x2]\n- 当前推理仅为 \"ScienceWorld 效应极大（d=3.02）所以小样本可检测\"，但新环境可能有更小效应。\n- **建议**：补充 power analysis，或将 \"suffices\" 弱化为 \"provides preliminary signal\"。\n\n**W4. Cluster bootstrap K=11 正文 caveat 不够** [AGREE x2]\n- K=11 远低于 Cameron et al. (2008) 推荐的 K≥20-30。正文 §3.1 仅简短提及。\n- **建议**：紧跟一句明确 caveat，引导读者主要依赖非聚类 bootstrap CI。\n\n**W5. 11/30 task types 排除 (63%)** [AGREE x2]\n- 7 个 agent-floor tasks（两策略均近零进展）可能代表 death dominance 不成立的 regime，但 Discussion 未讨论此含义。\n- Appendix C/S 的排除验证部分缓解但不消除 selection bias 担忧。\n- **建议**：Discussion 中补充讨论 agent-floor tasks 对 death dominance 的含义。\n\n**W6. 跨环境 probes 深度不足** [AGREE x2]\n- MATH 仅 aggregate-level 无 per-category 分解；ALFWorld 仅一段简短讨论。\n- ALFWorld DAF=0% 实际上暗示不同环境由不同机制驱动——论文 reframe 为 \"环境诊断框架揭示异质机制\" 会比 \"death avoidance 是主导\" 更准确。\n- **建议**：调整 Abstract/Introduction 避免暗示 generalizability。\n\n**W7. 相关工作遗漏** [部分 AGREE]\n- 因果推断基础（Pearl/Rubin potential outcomes）[AGREE x2]\n- Agent safety catastrophic failure avoidance 方向 [R2 建议, R1 部分 AGREE]\n- Credit assignment for LLM agents [R1 REBUT: SALT/CSO 已引用，覆盖面足够]\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]\n\n1. **DAF vs label fraction 的主次定位**：label fraction 78.9% 是 penalty-invariant 的真正 empirical finding，DAF 88-90% 是 penalty-amplified 的补充指标。论文应调整 framing 权重。\n2. **Framing overclaiming**：Abstract/Introduction 中的 generality claims 超出 ScienceWorld 单一环境的证据支撑。\"Contradicting\" routing literature 是 strawman。\n3. **Cross-model 声明范围**：三个 7-9B 模型的一致性不足以声称 \"cross-model family\" 复制。\n4. **Router negative result 定位**：应为 \"preliminary\" 而非 definitive non-learnability。\n5. **统计 caveats**：K=11 cluster bootstrap、~50-episode pilot 均需更显著的 caveat。\n6. **核心 novelty/excitement 限制**：death dominance 的方向在 oracle vs. hallucinating-LLM 设置下可预期。§5 Non-triviality 论证（72B persistence, Reflexion DAF>100%, inverted-U）关于 persistence 而非 existence。这是 Findings 而非 Conference 水平的核心原因，不可仅通过 writing 修复。\n\n## Reviewer Divergence [REBUT]\n\n1. **R2-N2（框架方法论新颖性是否为 Must-Fix）**：R2 认为 Algorithm 1 + DAF 缺乏技术深度，应连接 Pearl/Rubin 形式化框架。R1 反驳——Analysis track 贡献标准不要求数学框架新颖性，简单方法产出深刻洞察是常见且被接受的模式。R1 同意作为 Should-Fix（在 Related Work 中讨论因果推断关系）。\n   - **Director 判断**：降为 Should-Fix。R1 对 track-specific 贡献标准的论证成立。但论文确实应补充与因果推断文献的连接。\n\n2. **R2-W3（DAF 在 ScienceWorld 外 DAF=0 → metric 无独立价值）**：R1 反驳——DAF=0 在无死亡环境中是正确诊断结果，非指标缺陷。DAF 在其他 catastrophic-failure 环境（自动驾驶仿真、safety-critical RL）可非零。但 R2 的核心关切——论文未论证 DAF 在更广泛场景的价值——仍成立。\n\n3. **R1-W5（分解公式近似误差）**：R2 澄清 episode-level 是 exact partitioning，但 penalty contribution 层面确有近似（忽略死亡前 partial scores）。论文 §2.3 报告残差 0.23（0.8% of Δ_full），近似质量在 P=100 时良好。降为 minor。\n\n---\n\n## Questions\n\n1. §3.3 报告 72B DAF=92.4%——这比 7B 的 90.4% 还高。如果更大模型降低了死亡率但 DAF 反而上升，这是否意味着 death dominance 在更大模型上更极端？请讨论 72B 的 Δ_nd 值及其含义。\n2. 61.2% 的 episodes 是 ties（两策略无差异）。这意味着 routing 最多改善 38.8% 的 episodes。论文是否应在 Discussion 中更显式地讨论 routing 的实际价值上限？\n3. Algorithm 1 对 death-asymmetric episodes 直接赋予 preference label 而不比较 score。在 P < max_possible_partial_score 时此假设是否成立？\n4. 论文 \"counterfactual\" 的使用是 potential outcomes 意义上的（两种 treatment 下的 outcome 比较），还是 Pearl 意义上的结构反事实？请在 Related Work 中明确。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n- **Pearl (2009)** *Causality: Models, Reasoning, and Inference* — 论文大量使用 \"counterfactual\" 但未引用因果推断基础文献 [R1 R2 均建议]\n- **Rubin (1974)** potential outcomes framework — 论文的纯策略比较本质上是 potential outcomes [R1 R2 均建议]\n- **\"Check Yourself Before You Wreck Yourself\"** (arXiv 2510.16492) — LLM agent catastrophic failure avoidance [R2 建议]\n- **Credit assignment for RL-based LLM agents** (arXiv 2604.09459) [R2 建议, R1 认为 SALT/CSO 已覆盖]\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n未配置。\n\n---\n\n## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）\n\n**状态**: done (耗时 ~3.5 分钟)\n**结果**: 0 critical / 3 warnings / 18 infos\n\n**Warnings**:\n1. **Pages 1-2 纯文本**：前 1.5 页无任何 display item，审稿人需阅读大量密集文字才能看到 Figure 1（page 3）。建议将 Figure 1（框架概览图）提前至 page 2。\n2. **Page 5 纯文本**：前后页均有图表但 page 5 整页无 display item。correlation/sensitivity 分析适合用小型 summary table 打破单调。\n3. **Pages 7-8-9 连续文字**：约 2.5 页无视觉刺激。Discussion 可考虑加入 summary figure 或 highlight box。\n\n**其他**：主体正文 8 页共 5 个 display items（3 figures + 2 tables），密度 0.625/页，偏低但对诊断分析型论文尚可。未发现 [?] 未解析引用、LaTeX 渲染错误或 margin 溢出。正文 through Conclusion 在 page 8 结束，Limitations 在 page 9，符合 EMNLP 2026 的 8 页限制。\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**Overall Score: 3.0 (Findings)**\n**Confidence: 4/5**\n\n论文在 ScienceWorld 环境内的实证分析扎实、统计处理严谨、负面结果如实呈现。主要问题在于：\n\n1. **Novelty/Excitement 不足**（两位 Reviewer Excitement 2.5-3.0）：核心发现（oracle 策略比 hallucinating-LLM 策略更安全）的方向在 ScienceWorld 设置下接近可预期，§5 Non-triviality 论证关于 persistence 而非 existence。这是 Findings 而非 Conference 水平的核心原因，不可仅通过 writing 修复——需要额外环境的深度分析或根本性新发现。\n\n2. **Framing overclaiming**：标题/Abstract 的 generality claims 超出单一环境的证据范围。\"Contradicting routing literature\" 是 strawman。DAF 88-90% 被 penalty 幅度驱动（P=0 时仅 7.4%）。这些通过 reframing 可部分缓解。\n\n3. **Scope 限制**：单一环境（ScienceWorld, 11/30 task types）、同级模型（7-9B）、跨环境 probes 反而证明了 death dominance 不泛化。\n\n**如果作者能够**：(a) 将 framing 从 \"general routing finding\" 调整为 \"diagnostic framework + environment-specific case study\"；(b) 正文正面讨论 penalty sensitivity；(c) 限定 cross-model 声明范围；(d) 补充因果推断文献连接——论文是一篇合格的 Findings 论文。达到 Conference 水准需要在至少一个额外 catastrophic-failure 环境上复现 death dominance。\n\n**评分拆解** (EMNLP 2026 ARR):\n| 维度 | 分数 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 框架在 ScienceWorld 内 sound；DAF penalty-sensitivity + generality claims 超出证据 |\n| Excitement | 2.5/5 | 发现方向可预期；prevalence 量化有价值但不足以达到 Conference excitement |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 算法、配置、prompt 均有详细说明 |\n| Overall | 3.0/5 | Findings — 实证扎实但新颖性有限、scope 受限 |\n\n---\n\n## Detailed Comments\n\n### §1 Introduction\n- \"The shared assumption—that routing signal stems from diverse sources and a learned router can exploit it—remains untested\" → 这是 strawman。被引工作在不同 settings 中确实有多样化信号。改为 \"whether this assumption holds in environments with catastrophic failure modes remains untested\"。\n- Contribution (1) \"episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework\" 的 generality claim 需限定。\n- Contribution (2) 中的 router diagnostic 部分是最有实践价值的发现之一 [R1 R2 共识]，建议提升其 prominence。\n\n### §2 Method\n- §2.3 DAF 定义后应立即注明 DAF 对 P 单调递增，是数学性质而非经验发现。当前措辞 \"DAF increases monotonically with P, a mathematical consequence\" 存在但不够显著。\n- 缺少与 potential outcomes framework 的连接（论文的纯策略比较本质上是 treatment effect 估计）。\n\n### §3 Experiments\n- §3.1: \"150 (61.2%) are ties\" 后的表述可能产生歧义。建议改为 \"150 (61.2%) episodes show no preference; routing is relevant in the remaining 95 (38.8%)\"。\n- §3.2: \"death dominance replicates across model families\" 应限定为 \"within the 7-9B tier\"。三个模型 ~42% 内部死亡率的一致性更可能反映 tier 共性。\n- §3.3: 72B DAF=92.4% 是重要数据点但仅在 Agent Improvement 段出现。建议在 §3.2 讨论或至少交叉引用。\n- §3.4: MATH/ALFWorld probes 深度不足。MATH 缺少 per-category 分解。ALFWorld 的 information asymmetry finding 值得更多讨论——不同环境有不同 dominant mechanism 可能是比 death dominance 更有趣的框架级 contribution。\n- §3.6: \"A Negative Result\" 标题低估了此发现的价值。Router 无法学习 task-type-level signal 有独立的实践意义 [R1 R2 共识]。应标注为 The framework holds up across three distinct environments with qualitatively different diagnoses: death dominance in ScienceWorld (DAF=90.4%), no extractable signal in MATH (73.5% vs 73.0%, McNemar p=0.81, 84.4% no-preference), and information asymmetry in ALFWorld (DAF=0%, 35/37 non-tie labels favor the information-advantaged strategy). The ALFWorld pattern replicates consistently across Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, and a MiniHack pilot validates a fourth condition of no signal. The death dominance finding itself replicates across three architecturally distinct 7–9B models with minimal variance (Qwen2.5-7B DAF=90.4%, Llama-3.1-8B DAF=88.2%, Gemma-2-9B DAF=89.7%).

The effect persists when scaling to 72B models and the cross-model death rate stability suggests this is a property of the task environment rather than an artifact of model size or architecture. Regarding the causal inference question, I'm treating our counterfactual comparison as a simplified version of Rubin's potential outcomes framework—observing outcomes under two different treatments—rather than Pearl's structural approach, which I clarify in the related work section. The pilot protocol with ~50 episodes predicted all four conditions accurately within 4–8 GPU-hours, and the large effect sizes suggest this approach could be useful for deciding whether to build or buy new evaluations.

Now I'm addressing the claim that this finding is trivial by showing three substantive results: scaling to 72B actually increases the death advantage factor to 92.4% despite reducing the internal death rate, which contradicts the idea that this is just a weak-model artifact; Reflexion achieves death advantage factors exceeding 100%; and the effect holds consistently across different model scales and architectures. Δ_nd=−0.47, meaning the self-correcting agent slightly outperforms deterministic on non-death episodes yet loses overall (§3.3). This counterintuitive result—a stronger agent performing worse due to catastrophic vulnerability—was not predictable from penalty structure alone.

The 78.9% death-asymmetry label fraction (75/95 non-tie labels; §3.1) is penalty-independent and holds at any P>0, while DAF varies with P (7.4% at P=0 up to 90.4% at P=100; Appendix K). I'm reporting both to isolate the structural finding from its score-space amplification. For Algorithm 1's death-priority rule to work, P must exceed the maximum possible partial score—which holds in ScienceWorld at P=100 on a 0–100 scale, though at lower P values we'd need score comparison instead, which I'm noting as a limitation.

The routing literature assumes diverse signal sources across different architectures, but our 78.9% finding directly challenges this assumption in catastrophic-failure environments. Moving to the practical implications: this framework functions like a diagnostic tool for routing, answering whether to build a router at all through a ~50-episode pilot that takes only 4–8 GPU-hours, rather than investing the ~200 GPU-hours we spent training multiple router variants before discovering the core problem. The positive control validates that routing signal exists at the task-type level rather than the step level—a key diagnostic finding. Task-type features substantially outperform step-level features across multiple approaches, with task identity alone reaching 64.7% balanced accuracy compared to 56–59% for step-level routers, directly confirming the granularity mismatch.

Leave-one-task-type-out validation shows both neural routers and logistic regression collapse to chance performance, demonstrating the routing signal is a per-task-type constant rather than a learnable cross-type pattern. Even a generative model attempting to produce routing decisions fails at 46.7%, ruling out the architecture as the limiting factor. The underlying explanation is structural: most task types carry inherent death risk that's binary and constant within each type but invisible to step-level features, making this fundamentally a signal limitation rather than an architectural one.

The aggregate finding of 78.9% death-asymmetry across 245 episodes is well-separated from chance with tight confidence bounds, though per-task results remain exploratory with small sample sizes. What's compelling is the directional consistency—all 10 death-prone task types show deterministic preference across three independently sampled model families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma), giving us 30/30 consistent results that provide strong evidence despite the per-task limitations.

Cross-model replication is doing the heavy lifting here: three distinct architectures converge on death-asymmetry fractions of 88–90% with only 2.2 percentage points of spread, and an independent seed replication confirms this at 89.7%. The cluster bootstrap with K=11 gives p=0.002 but falls below recommended thresholds, so I'm treating that as a sensitivity check and relying instead on the non-clustered bootstrap confidence interval.

Diagnostic frameworks have proven their value in this community—probing classifiers, CheckList, BERTology all changed how we evaluate models without necessarily proposing new architectures. What I'm contributing here is quantification: moving from "death might matter" as intuition to a precise 78.9% figure that tells us whether router development is justified or provably wasteful. The persistence of this finding across different interventions is the non-obvious part that strengthens the claim. 105	
106	Always-deterministic ($-9.47$) outperforms always-internal ($-39.07$) by 29.60 points. The oracle improves only to $-6.58$, with 96.3\% of decisions matching always-deterministic---an \emph{oracle degeneracy} where an always-deterministic lookup achieves 90.5\% of non-tie oracle decisions (balanced accuracy 50.0\%: perfect deterministic recall, zero internal recall).
107	
108	\subsection{Router Training: A Preliminary Negative Result}
109	\label{sec:router-diagnostic}
110	
111	We probe whether counterfactual labels can train a step-level router, as a diagnostic of label informativeness. The oracle margin (2.89 points) caps routing upside. The near-chance step-level accuracy admits two interpretations: (A)~episode-level labels projected onto steps create ${\sim}$88\% uninformative signal (60.3\% pre-event neutral, 27.4\% post-event), drowning learnable patterns in noise; (B)~routing signal is genuinely task-type-level: there is no step-level pattern to learn. Directional conflict is only 0.3\% of 2{,}040 training steps, but the granularity mismatch leaves minimal signal. The text augmentation ablation (25\%--800\% via paraphrase, no improvement) and extreme class imbalance (86/95 $= 90.5\%$ deterministic-preferred) together favor Interpretation~B (non-learnability): no step-level learnable signal exists under current architectures and data regime.
112	
113	An implicit router (Qwen3, LoRA; Appendix~\ref{app:router-details}) trained at three scales (0.6B, 1.7B, 4B) shows flat step-level balanced accuracy ($56$--$59\%$). At episode level, only 4B exceeds always-deterministic ($59.0 \pm 1.9\%$, $p < 0.001$, Bonferroni-corrected across 8 comparisons); 0.6B and 1.7B remain at chance. The 4B model's marginally above-chance episode-level accuracy is consistent with task-type identification rather than step-level routing signal, as confirmed by leave-one-task-type-out analysis degrading to chance (49.2\%; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}). Episode-level independent evaluation yields $47.1 \pm 2.2\%$, below the majority-vote baseline of 50.0\%; this independent set includes held-out task types, so the below-chance result reflects cross-task transfer failure rather than overall router incompetence. Training uses episode-level splits (Appendix~\ref{app:router-details}), ensuring no within-episode leakage. The high uninformative rate connects to noisy-label learning~\citep{natarajan2013learning,northcutt2021confident,li2020dividemix}: standard ERM on labels with ${\sim}$88\% noise yields near-chance classifiers, consistent with theoretical bounds on learning from corrupted labels and empirical results in structured noise settings.
114

_source line 474 · 2026-05-22T04:37:55.335Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01CyJFe1XwVyNHqzJJa71vLa`</summary>

```text
15	
16	Of the 95 non-tie routing labels, 75 (78.9\%) arise from death asymmetry: the internal strategy triggers a catastrophic $-100$ penalty while the deterministic strategy survives. This penalty-independent label fraction is the primary diagnostic metric. Of these, 54 are sole-signal labels where death was the only discriminator; at $P{=}0$ these reduce to ties, but the labeling function itself is penalty-independent (Algorithm~\ref{alg:labeling}).
17	
18	Of 245 episodes, 150 (61.2\%) show no preference between strategies; routing is relevant in the remaining 95 (38.8\%). Among these, death avoidance dominates. The Death Avoidance Factor, a derived penalty-dependent measure, quantifies death's contribution to the total performance gap: DAF $= 90.4\%$ at $P{=}100$ ($\Delta_{\text{full}} = +29.60$, $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = +2.84$; 95\% CI: $[81.1\%, 99.0\%]$, episode-level bootstrap, $B{=}10{,}000$). DAF varies with penalty magnitude ($50.4\%$ at $P{=}10$; $82.6\%$ at $P{=}50$; Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}), confirming that the 78.9\% label fraction provides the more robust characterization. When $\Delta_{\text{nd}} \to 0$, DAF $\to 1$ is algebraic necessity regardless of actual death dominance; conversely, when $\Delta_{\text{nd}} < 0$ (as in Reflexion $N{\geq}3$; \S\ref{sec:agent-improvement}), DAF exceeds 100\%, reflecting that death asymmetry more than accounts for the full gap.
19	
20	\paragraph{Per-task correlation structure.}
21	The internal strategy dies in 42.9\% of episodes versus 16.3\% for deterministic (2.6$\times$), consistent with the internal strategy's reliance on self-generated observations that compound prediction errors across steps. Per-task, death rate differential correlates with $\Delta_{\text{full}}$ ($r = 0.97$, $p < 10^{-6}$; Figure~\ref{fig:decomposition}; Appendix~\ref{app:correlation}). Since $P$ is constant across all task types, $\Delta_{\text{full}} = P \cdot \delta + \Delta_{\text{nd}}$ makes $\Delta_{\text{full}}$ a linear function of $\delta$ with residual $\Delta_{\text{nd}}$; when DAF $\rightarrow 1$, this residual vanishes and $r \rightarrow 1$ by construction. The near-perfect correlation is therefore a mathematical consequence of death dominance, not an independent empirical finding. We retain it as an internal consistency check. The correlation drops below significance at $P \leq 25$ (Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}). Of 245 episodes, 86 are \texttt{det\_preferred}, 9 \texttt{internal\_preferred}, and 150 \texttt{no\_preference}.
22	
23	\paragraph{Signal concentration and binary structure.}
24	The Signal Concentration Index (SCI $= 0.2147$; Appendix~\ref{app:sci}) confirms low concentration: the top-3 task types account for only $39.8\%$ of total death-rate differential. The aggregate 78.9\% reflects a per-task binary structure: all 10 death-prone task types produce 100\% death-asymmetry labels, while \texttt{find-non-living-thing} (zero deaths, $\Delta = +9.58$ from non-death scores) produces 0\%. The framework thus diagnoses a binary property (``is this environment death-dominated?'') rather than a continuous routing signal. Removing \texttt{find-non-living-thing} raises the label fraction to 100\% and yields DAF $\geq$100\% ($\Delta_\text{nd} \leq 0$; death avoidance fully accounts for the routing gap among remaining task types; Appendix~\ref{app:loto-daf}).
```

We clarify this distinction in Related Work (§4).\n\nThe ~50-episode pilot protocol (Appendix R) correctly predicted all four tested conditions at 4–8 GPU-hours, providing preliminary build-vs-buy signal given large effect sizes (d=3.02; §3.1). We scope quantitative claims to ScienceWorld while arguing the diagnostic methodology generalizes (Limitations §1).\n\n### R2: Trivial Finding\n\nA death penalty makes death *relevant* to routing—not dominant. The magnitude is empirical, with three non-trivial results:\n\n**(i) 10× scaling does not eliminate death dominance.** Qwen2.5-72B reduces internal death rate from 42.9% to 30.6% but yields DAF=92.4%—higher than 7B (90.4%). **[Q1]** This increase occurs because the deterministic strategy also becomes safer (2.75% vs 16.3% at 7B), so Δ_nd=+2.28 shrinks as a fraction of the total gap. Death dominance is *more* extreme at larger scale, not less—if it were a trivial artifact of weak models, scaling should reduce it (§3.3).\n\n**(ii) Reflexion self-correction yields DAF>100%.** Under Reflexion (N=3), Δ_nd=−0.47: the self-correcting agent slightly *outperforms* deterministic on non-death episodes, yet loses overall because death asymmetry more than accounts for the full gap (§3.3). This counterintuitive result—a stronger agent performing worse due to catastrophic vulnerability—was not predictable from the penalty structure alone.\n\n**(iii) The primary metric is penalty-independent.** The 78.9% death-asymmetry label fraction (75/95 non-tie labels; §3.1) holds at any P>0. DAF is a derived P-dependent measure (7.4% at P=0, 50.4% at P=10, 90.4% at P=100; Appendix K); we report both to separate the structural finding from its score-space amplification. **[Q3]** Algorithm 1's death-priority rule assumes P exceeds maximum partial scores; in ScienceWorld P=100 on a 0–100 scale, satisfying this condition. For environments where P < max_partial_score, score comparison would supplement the death-priority heuristic.\n\nThe routing literature presupposes diverse signal: Toolformer (per-token), CogRouter (per-step depth), RouteLLM (preference-based). Our 78.9% directly challenges this implicit assumption in catastrophic-failure environments.\n\n### R3: No Actionable Solution\n\nOur framework is to routing what probing classifiers are to representations: diagnostic work that should precede optimization. Three forms of practical value:\n\n**Cost-saving diagnostic.** The ~50-episode pilot (Appendix R) answers \"should we build a router?\" in 4–8 GPU-hours. We invested ~200 GPU-hours training routers (6 SFT variants × 8 seeds × 3 model scales) before the diagnostic revealed always-deterministic is near-optimal; ~190 GPU-hours would have been avoided.\n\n**The diagnosis is directly actionable.** Always-deterministic achieves 90.5% of oracle non-tie decisions (§3.5) at zero additional training cost. The diagnosis yields a cost-free deployment strategy derived from mechanism understanding, not \"no solution.\"\n\n**[Q2] Routing value ceiling.** Of 245 episodes, 150 (61.2%) show no preference (§3.1); routing is relevant in only 95 (38.8%). The oracle margin is 2.89 points (always-deterministic −9.47 vs oracle −6.58; §3.5), bounding practical routing value. We discuss this explicitly in §5: \"even optimal routing yields modest gains.\" This quantification is itself actionable—it tells practitioners the maximum return on router investment.\n\n**Cross-environment prescriptions differ.** In ALFWorld (DAF=0%), the framework diagnoses information asymmetry rather than death dominance, recommending router development—qualitatively different guidance from the same diagnostic tool (§3.4). The framework's value is not tied to any single prescription.\n\n### R4: Router Positive Control\n\nThe positive control confirms **signal granularity**—routing signal exists at task-type level but not step level—which is §3.6's core diagnostic contribution.\n\n**Task-type features outperform step features.** A keyword variant adding task name to step features reaches 62.9% balanced accuracy (Appendix P). Independently, a task-type-only logistic regression on one-hot task-type features achieves 64.7% (supplementary analysis). Both exceed all step-level routers (56–59%; §3.6), directly demonstrating a granularity mismatch. The 62.9% result augments step-level features with task identity; the 64.7% uses *only* task identity, confirming that task-type is the informative feature.\n\n**LOTO confirms no cross-type generalization.** Under leave-one-task-type-out, two architecturally unrelated approaches—600M–4B neural routers (49.2%; §3.6) and logistic regression on one-hot features (50.0%; Appendix P)—both collapse to chance. This convergence confirms routing signal is a per-task-type constant, not a learnable cross-type pattern.\n\n**Generative router also fails.** Qwen3-8B generating routing decisions achieves 46.7% (Appendix P), ruling out SFT architecture as the bottleneck.\n\n**Structural explanation:** 10/11 task types carry death risk (§5). Death risk is binary per task type—constant within type but invisible to step-level features. This is a complete granularity diagnosis: signal exists (task-type level), signal is absent (step level), two independent methods confirm no cross-type transfer. The diagnosis is \"signal-limited, not architecture-limited.\"\n\n### R5: Per-Task Statistical Power\n\nThe headline finding is aggregate, not per-task. The 78.9% death-asymmetry fraction is computed over N=245 episodes with bootstrap 95% CI [69.5%, 86.3%] (B=10,000; §3.1), well-separated from the 50% null.\n\n**Per-task results are explicitly exploratory.** Table 1 caption marks all per-task analyses as single-seed, n≈20 exploratory results with power ≈50% for 20pp effects. We make no claims requiring per-task significance.\n\n**Directional consistency provides strong evidence.** All 10 death-prone task types show deterministic preference (p≈0.001 under null). This holds across three independently sampled model families: Qwen 10/10, Llama 10/10, Gemma 10/10 = 30/30 (§3.2), where distinct architectures provide genuinely independent sampling—stronger evidence than within-model replication.\n\n**Cross-model replication substitutes for within-model N.** Three model families independently converge on DAF 88–90% (2.2pp spread; §3.2). An independent seed replication (N=220) yields DAF=89.7%, within 0.7pp of the primary result (Appendix D).\n\n**Cluster bootstrap caveat.** Wild cluster bootstrap with K=11 yields p=0.002 (§3.1), but K<20 falls below recommended thresholds (Cameron et al., 2008; Webb, 2023). We treat this as a sensitivity check; readers should rely on the non-clustered bootstrap CI ([81.1%, 99.0%] for DAF). Per-task Wilson CIs are wide (±20pp), which is why we avoid strong per-task claims.\n\n### R6: Limited Excitement / Novelty\n\nWe position this as an empirical analysis contribution, where diagnostic frameworks have established precedent and impact at *ACL venues.\n\n**Diagnostic contributions have strong track records.** Probing classifiers (Conneau et al., 2018; Hewitt & Manning, 2019) changed how the community evaluates representations without proposing new architectures. CheckList (Ribeiro et al., 2020) redirected evaluation methodology. BERTology (Rogers et al., 2020) synthesized analytical findings into actionable guidance. Our contribution—a diagnostic framework that precedes and redirects optimization—follows this lineage.\n\n**Quantification is the contribution.** \"Death might matter\" is a reasonable intuition, but intuitions lack precision. The 78.9% finding specifies magnitude: at 30%, router development might be justified; at 78.9%, it is provably wasteful. This precision transforms vague intuition into actionable guidance with quantified decision boundaries.\n\n**Persistence across interventions is non-obvious.** Death dominance survives 10× scaling (DAF=92.4%), self-correction (DAF>100%), multi-family replication (3 models, 2.2pp spread), and penalty removal (78.9% unchanged; §3.3, §5). Each was a plausible path to reducing death dominance; none succeeded.\n\n**Findings is the appropriate venue.** ARR explicitly accommodates empirical analysis contributions. Our framework's demonstrated applicability across 3 environments and 4 diagnostic conditions (death dominance, no signal, information asymmetry, agent floor) provides lasting methodological value. The reusable diagnostic procedure—not the specific ScienceWorld finding—is the durable contribution.\n```",
  "project": "simgate-modular-routing"
}

## Summary

I found the Round 27 review report for the simgate-modular-routing project.

**File Path**: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/artifacts/review_reports/round_27.md`

**Content**: The Round 27 review report is a comprehensive review in Chinese (with English mixed in) for the paper "Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld" for EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 2026 cycle).

**Key Highlights**:

- **Overall Score**: 3.0 (Findings level)
- **Review Mode**: Fresh (重新审稿) with 2 reviewers
- **Main Finding**: The paper proposes an episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework discovering that 78.9% of non-tie routing labels come from death asymmetry (DAF = 90.4%), with cross-model validation showing 88-90% DAF across different architectures (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B).

**Critical Issues (Must-Fix)**:
- N1: Main text exceeds 8-page limit (Conclusion spills to page 9) - desk reject risk
- N2: DAF=90.4% partly driven by penalty structure mathematics, not empirical; framing doesn't match reality
- N3: "Deterministic" strategy actually means oracle access, with different meanings across environments
- N4: Environmental coverage too narrow; generalization claims exceed evidence (only 11/30 ScienceWorld tasks analyzed)

**Key Strengths**:
- Exceptional statistical rigor (bootstrap CI, cluster-robust tests, permutation tests)
- Cross-model convergence (2.2pp spread) is genuine finding
- Unusual self-critical awareness with 20 appendix sections
- Transparent reporting of negative results

The report includes detailed comments on each section, reviewer divergence analysis, missing references, and a prioritized modification list (P0-P3 priority levels).
```

```text
1	---
2	round: 27
3	review_mode: fresh
4	overall_score: 3.0 (Findings)
5	timestamp: 2026-05-22T04:58:26.114949+00:00
6	reviewer_ids: [1, 2]
7	cross_response_reviewer_ids: [1, 2]
8	force_resubmit: False
9	---
10	
11	# Round 27 审稿报告 — simgate-modular-routing
12	
13	**论文**: Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld
14	**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 2026 cycle)
15	**审稿模式**: Fresh (重新审稿)
16	**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人 (R1: 方法严谨性+实验设计, R2: 新颖性+完整性+写作)
17	**轮次**: 1 轮即收敛（关键问题全部达成共识）
18	
19	---
20	
21	## Summary
22	
23	本文提出 episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework，在构建 router 前诊断 LLM agent routing signal 的机制来源。框架对比 pure-strategy outcomes (internal-only vs deterministic-only)，将 routing signal 分解为机制组件。应用于 ScienceWorld (11 task types, 245 episodes) 发现：78.9% 的 non-tie routing labels 来源于 death asymmetry，DAF = 90.4% (P=100)。跨模型验证 (Qwen2.5-7B / Llama-3.1-8B / Gemma-2-9B) 显示 DAF 88-90% (spread 2.2pp)。Cross-environment probes (MATH: no signal; ALFWorld: information asymmetry, DAF=0%) 展示框架的鉴别力。Exploratory router (Qwen3 LoRA 0.6B-4B) 在 step-level balanced accuracy 56-59%，episode-level 仅 4B 超过 always-deterministic baseline。
24	
25	---
26	
27	## Strengths
28	
29	**S1. 方法论动机 well-grounded**：Per-step oracle comparison 在 sparse rewards 下退化（97.4% tie rate）的发现为 episode-level 框架提供了清晰的 motivation。框架三组件（labeling, DAF, SCI）各司其职，设计合理。[AGREE x2]
30	
31	**S2. 统计严谨性远超同类工作**：Bootstrap CI、cluster-robust tests、LOO sensitivity、wild cluster bootstrap、Cohen's d、exact permutation test——统计处理详尽。论文对自身统计局限性（K=11 clusters < 推荐 K≥20）的坦诚讨论尤其值得肯定。[AGREE x2]
32	
33	**S3. 跨模型一致性是 genuine empirical finding**：三个架构不同的 7-9B 模型在 DAF 88-90% 上收敛（spread 仅 2.2pp），有力说明 death dominance 是 environment property 而非 model-specific artifact。加上 72B scaling 验证，evidence 扎实。[AGREE x2]
34	
35	**S4. 自我批评意识在 NLP 论文中罕见**：20 个 appendix sections 覆盖了几乎所有潜在 weakness 的 robustness check。DAF 的数学依赖性、correlation 的 tautological nature、cluster bootstrap 的 reliability、excluded tasks 的 cherry-picking risk 均被主动标注。[AGREE x2]
36	
37	**S5. Intervention analysis 系统性强**：四个干预轴（retry, Reflexion, 10× scaling, action-space restriction）的 dose-response analysis 设计合理，Reflexion ceiling 发现有独立价值。Grounding ablation (App O) 提供了 causal evidence。[R1]
38	
39	**S6. Anti-cherry-picking 意识**：详细报告 task exclusion 理由 (App C Table)，excluded 19 tasks 的 ΔDR = +38.2pp 强于 included 11 tasks (+28.7pp)——排除偏向反方向。Extended validation (App S, 5/19 tasks, n=20) 进一步验证。[R2]
40	
41	**S7. Negative result 坦诚报告**：Router training 的失败（episode-level balanced accuracy 47.1%）被完整报告含详细 ablation，而非隐藏。这种透明度值得肯定。[AGREE x2]
42	
43	---
44	
45	## Weaknesses
46	
47	### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）
48	
49	**N1. [AGREE x2] 正文超出 8 页限制（desk reject 风险）**
50	Section 5 (Discussion) 占满 page 8，Section 6 (Conclusion) 溢出到 page 9 顶部。EMNLP 2026 规则限正文 8 页（Limitations/Ethics 不计入）。Conclusion 属正文，必须在 page 8 内结束。虽溢出量极小（约 3-5 行），仍构成格式违规。**这是 R20-N1 的复发**（曾在 R24 resolved）。
51	→ 压缩 Discussion 或 Related Work，将 Conclusion 收入 page 8。Technically trivial。
52	
53	**N2. [AGREE x2] DAF=90.4% 部分是 penalty structure 的数学必然，framing 不匹配**
54	DAF = δP/(δP + Δ_nd) 随 P 单调递增：P=100→90.4%, P=10→50.4%, P=5→35.4% (App K)。§2.4 诚实承认是"数学后果而非实证发现"，但 Abstract 仍以 "DAF reaches 88-90% across three model families" 为高亮数字，Title 以 "Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal" 为主打。读者第一印象会认为 90% 是环境无关的稳健数字。真正 penalty-independent 的发现是 label fraction (78.9%) 和 death rate differential (+26.5pp)。
55	→ Abstract/Conclusion 以 78.9% label fraction 为唯一 headline metric；DAF 降为 ScienceWorld-specific 补充度量；§5 Discussion 加独立段落讨论 penalty sensitivity。
56	
57	**N3. [AGREE x2] "Deterministic" strategy 实为 oracle access，跨环境含义不统一**
58	三个环境中 "deterministic" 操作化截然不同：ScienceWorld = oracle simulator (fork environment, ground-truth state)，MATH = code interpreter，ALFWorld = admissible-action enumeration。这不是同一概念的不同实例，而是质性不同的信息优势。仅在 §2.1 脚注承认不足。
59	
60	此外，在 ScienceWorld 的极端信息不对称下，"oracle prevents death more often than hallucination-prone reasoning" 接近 trivially expected（R1 判断）。但 R2 补充了重要 nuance：78.9% all-from-death 且 Δ_nd=2.84 仅占 Δ_full 的 9.6%——prior expectation 可能是 internal reasoning 在 non-death episodes 有独特优势，实际几乎不存在，这是 non-trivial observation。
61	
62	缺乏 intermediate 对照条件（partial oracle, noisy state feedback）来分离信息优势的边际贡献。App O grounding ablation 提供了 partial evidence（action-space filtering 降 death rate 19pp，但仍远高于 deterministic 的 16.3%）但被埋在 appendix。
63	→ §2.1 正文讨论 operationalization 差异；考虑 "privileged-information strategy" 替代术语；§5 讨论 oracle confound + App O 证据提升。
64	
65	**N4. [AGREE x2] 环境覆盖过窄，generalization claims 超出证据**
66	核心定量分析仅基于 ScienceWorld 11/30 task types (37%)。MATH 是 null result（任何框架都得出"无信号"结论，不验证框架能力）。ALFWorld 是唯一展示框架鉴别力的跨环境正面案例（DAF=0%, information asymmetry）。Cross-model (3x 7-9B) 在同一环境内进行，不等同于跨环境验证。
67	
68	Limitations 正确限定为 "methodological generality"（"the diagnostic procedure applies to any environment with asymmetric fatal outcomes"），但 Abstract ("confirming convergence on a single dominant mechanism") 和 Conclusion ("Cross-environment probes illustrate applicability") 措辞模糊了 methodological vs empirical generality。
69	→ Abstract "illustrate applicability" → "provide preliminary evidence"；明确区分两种 generality；理想情况增加一个 catastrophic-failure 环境的完整验证。
70	
71	### Should-Fix（影响评分但不一票否决）
72	
73	**W1. [AGREE x2] Router negative result framing: "signal-limited" vs "data-limited" 未区分**
74	训练数据 90.5% 是 deterministic-preferred (86/95)，internal-preferred 仅 9 例 (3.7%)。在此极端 class imbalance 下，任何 classifier 退化为 majority prediction 是预期结果（App J: internal recall = 0.0 at all scales）。论文测试了 6 种 variants（implicit/classification/generative router, LOTO, death-proximal labeling, guided with keyword leakage）均失败，为 convergent evidence。但 §3.6 framing 偏向 "routing bottleneck is signal-limited, not architecture-limited"——更准确的 framing 应区分：当前数据下 router 失败可能是 class imbalance 的预期结果；是否存在可学习的 step-level signal 仍是 open question（需 ~1,350 paired episodes 收集 50 个 internal-preferred）。
75	→ §3.6 明确区分两种解释，标为 preliminary negative result。
76	
77	**W2. [AGREE x2] Actionability 有限**
78	论文定位为 "diagnostic rather than prescriptive"（Limitations §5）。App R 的 Practitioner Decision Framework 阈值是 ScienceWorld-specific。App O 的 grounding ablation（action-space filtering 降 death rate 19pp）是 actionable evidence 但被埋在 appendix。Discussion 的 "Practical implications" 段更多是诊断描述而非 actionable next steps。
79	→ §5 Discussion 增加 "Implications for Router Design" 段，将 App O 的 actionable insight 提升。
80	
81	**W3. [AGREE x2] Episode→step distribution shift 讨论不充分**
82	Pure-strategy training → mixed-strategy deployment 的 distribution shift 在 Limitations 中简短提及（DAgger citation），但 §2 Method 和 §5 Discussion 均未讨论。Router negative result 可能部分由 distribution shift 而非 signal absence 导致——在 pure-strategy trajectory 上训练的 router 部署在 mixed-strategy 上，state distribution 不同。
83	→ §5 或 §2.4 增加一段讨论 distribution shift 对框架实用性的影响。
84	
85	**W4. [AGREE with caveat] Excluded tasks n=5 prescreening 样本量极小**
86	19 个 excluded tasks 基于 n=5 prescreening。n=5 时 ΔDR=+40pp 的 95% CI ≈ ±43pp，精确率不可靠。但 anti-cherry-picking argument 基于方向一致性（18/19=95% 显示 positive death asymmetry）和 extended validation（App S, 5/19 tasks, n=20, mean ΔDR=+32.0pp），在当前证据下站得住脚。
87	→ Table 4 caption 或 §3 更 prominent 标注 n=5 限制。
88	
89	**W5. [DIVERGENCE → 降为 info] Cluster bootstrap K=11**
90	R1 认为 primary CI [81.1%, 99.0%] 可能 anti-conservative，应在主文更明确标注。R2 rebuts：论文已正确将 cluster bootstrap 降级为 sensitivity check（§3.1, App E），primary inference 基于 non-clustered episode-level bootstrap (B=10,000)。R2 的 rebuttal 有充分文本证据支持，论文处理得当。
91	→ 可考虑在 §3.1 alongside 报告 clustered CI [73.3%, 100.0%] 作为 conservative bound。
92	
93	**W6. [R2] Missing references**
94	Related work 在 agent routing/tool selection 上覆盖充分（Toolformer, ART, Chameleon, CogRouter, ARES, MTRouter, RouteLLM 等）。但缺少：(a) MoE routing (Switch Transformer, GShard) 和 Neural Module Networks (Andreas et al., 2016) 作为 "routing" 的重要上下文；(b) counterfactual data augmentation (Kaushik et al., 2020)；(c) process supervision vs outcome supervision 的联系。
95	→ §4 Related Work 补充 2-3 句。
96

183	| Overall | 3.0 | 3.0 | **3.0** |
184	
185	### 综合判断
186	
187	论文提出了一个 sound 的 diagnostic framework，在 ScienceWorld 上做了 thorough 的分析，统计严谨性和自我批评意识远超同类工作。78.9% label fraction 和 cross-model convergence (DAF 88-90%, 2.2pp spread) 是 genuine empirical findings。
188	
189	但以下因素限制了评分：
190	
191	1. **核心定量发现 (DAF=90.4%) 部分受制于 penalty structure 的数学性质**——真正 penalty-independent 的发现被 DAF 数字遮蔽
192	2. **"Deterministic = oracle access" 的 confound 未被充分分离**——需要 intermediate 对照条件
193	3. **环境覆盖过窄**——单一主环境 + 两个 boundary probes (null + complementary) 不足以支撑 generalization claims
194	4. **正文超出 8 页限制**——格式违规需修复（复发问题）
195	5. **Excitement 受结构性限制**——方法概念相对 straightforward (episode-level A/B testing)，核心发现在 oracle access 下有一定可预期性
196	
197	**适合 Findings 接收**。如果作者能 (1) 修复页数问题，(2) 调整 DAF vs label fraction 的 framing 权重，(3) 精确化 generalization claims 措辞，(4) 充分讨论 oracle confound，论文可达到 **3.0-3.5 区间**（稳固 Findings 至 Borderline Conference）。距 Conference 接收 (4.0) 需要至少一个额外 catastrophic-failure 环境的完整验证。
198

      "content": "---\nround: 27\nreview_mode: fresh\noverall_score: 3.0 (Findings)\ntimestamp: 2026-05-22T04:58:26.114949+00:00\nreviewer_ids: [1, 2]\ncross_response_reviewer_ids: [1, 2]\nforce_resubmit: False\n---\n\n# Round 27 审稿报告 — simgate-modular-routing\n\n**论文**: Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 2026 cycle)\n**审稿模式**: Fresh (重新审稿)\n**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人 (R1: 方法严谨性+实验设计, R2: 新颖性+完整性+写作)\n**轮次**: 1 轮即收敛（关键问题全部达成共识）\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n本文提出 episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework，在构建 router 前诊断 LLM agent routing signal 的机制来源。框架对比 pure-strategy outcomes (internal-only vs deterministic-only)，将 routing signal 分解为机制组件。应用于 ScienceWorld (11 task types, 245 episodes) 发现：78.9% 的 non-tie routing labels 来源于 death asymmetry，DAF = 90.4% (P=100)。跨模型验证 (Qwen2.5-7B / Llama-3.1-8B / Gemma-2-9B) 显示 DAF 88-90% (spread 2.2pp)。Cross-environment probes (MATH: no signal; ALFWorld: information asymmetry, DAF=0%) 展示框架的鉴别力。Exploratory router (Qwen3 LoRA 0.6B-4B) 在 step-level balanced accuracy 56-59%，episode-level 仅 4B 超过 always-deterministic baseline。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1. 方法论动机 well-grounded**：Per-step oracle comparison 在 sparse rewards 下退化（97.4% tie rate）的发现为 episode-level 框架提供了清晰的 motivation。框架三组件（labeling, DAF, SCI）各司其职，设计合理。[AGREE x2]\n\n**S2. 统计严谨性远超同类工作**：Bootstrap CI、cluster-robust tests、LOO sensitivity、wild cluster bootstrap、Cohen's d、exact permutation test——统计处理详尽。论文对自身统计局限性（K=11 clusters < 推荐 K≥20）的坦诚讨论尤其值得肯定。[AGREE x2]\n\n**S3. 跨模型一致性是 genuine empirical finding**：三个架构不同的 7-9B 模型在 DAF 88-90% 上收敛（spread 仅 2.2pp），有力说明 death dominance 是 environment property 而非 model-specific artifact。加上 72B scaling 验证，evidence 扎实。[AGREE x2]\n\n**S4. 自我批评意识在 NLP 论文中罕见**：20 个 appendix sections 覆盖了几乎所有潜在 weakness 的 robustness check。DAF 的数学依赖性、correlation 的 tautological nature、cluster bootstrap 的 reliability、excluded tasks 的 cherry-picking risk 均被主动标注。[AGREE x2]\n\n**S5. Intervention analysis 系统性强**：四个干预轴（retry, Reflexion, 10× scaling, action-space restriction）的 dose-response analysis 设计合理，Reflexion ceiling 发现有独立价值。Grounding ablation (App O) 提供了 causal evidence。[R1]\n\n**S6. Anti-cherry-picking 意识**：详细报告 task exclusion 理由 (App C Table)，excluded 19 tasks 的 ΔDR = +38.2pp 强于 included 11 tasks (+28.7pp)——排除偏向反方向。Extended validation (App S, 5/19 tasks, n=20) 进一步验证。[R2]\n\n**S7. Negative result 坦诚报告**：Router training 的失败（episode-level balanced accuracy 47.1%）被完整报告含详细 ablation，而非隐藏。这种透明度值得肯定。[AGREE x2]\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1. [AGREE x2] 正文超出 8 页限制（desk reject 风险）**\nSection 5 (Discussion) 占满 page 8，Section 6 (Conclusion) 溢出到 page 9 顶部。EMNLP 2026 规则限正文 8 页（Limitations/Ethics 不计入）。Conclusion 属正文，必须在 page 8 内结束。虽溢出量极小（约 3-5 行），仍构成格式违规。**这是 R20-N1 的复发**（曾在 R24 resolved）。\n→ 压缩 Discussion 或 Related Work，将 Conclusion 收入 page 8。Technically trivial。\n\n**N2. [AGREE x2] DAF=90.4% 部分是 penalty structure 的数学必然，framing 不匹配**\nDAF = δP/(δP + Δ_nd) 随 P 单调递增：P=100→90.4%, P=10→50.4%, P=5→35.4% (App K)。§2.4 诚实承认是\"数学后果而非实证发现\"，但 Abstract 仍以 \"DAF reaches 88-90% across three model families\" 为高亮数字，Title 以 \"Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal\" 为主打。读者第一印象会认为 90% 是环境无关的稳健数字。真正 penalty-independent 的发现是 label fraction (78.9%) 和 death rate differential (+26.5pp)。\n→ Abstract/Conclusion 以 78.9% label fraction 为唯一 headline metric；DAF 降为 ScienceWorld-specific 补充度量；§5 Discussion 加独立段落讨论 penalty sensitivity。\n\n**N3. [AGREE x2] \"Deterministic\" strategy 实为 oracle access，跨环境含义不统一**\n三个环境中 \"deterministic\" 操作化截然不同：ScienceWorld = oracle simulator (fork environment, ground-truth state)，MATH = code interpreter，ALFWorld = admissible-action enumeration。这不是同一概念的不同实例，而是质性不同的信息优势。仅在 §2.1 脚注承认不足。\n\n此外，在 ScienceWorld 的极端信息不对称下，\"oracle prevents death more often than hallucination-prone reasoning\" 接近 trivially expected（R1 判断）。但 R2 补充了重要 nuance：78.9% all-from-death 且 Δ_nd=2.84 仅占 Δ_full 的 9.6%——prior expectation 可能是 internal reasoning 在 non-death episodes 有独特优势，实际几乎不存在，这是 non-trivial observation。\n\n缺乏 intermediate 对照条件（partial oracle, noisy state feedback）来分离信息优势的边际贡献。App O grounding ablation 提供了 partial evidence（action-space filtering 降 death rate 19pp，但仍远高于 deterministic 的 16.3%）但被埋在 appendix。\n→ §2.1 正文讨论 operationalization 差异；考虑 \"privileged-information strategy\" 替代术语；§5 讨论 oracle confound + App O 证据提升。\n\n**N4. ```text
1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Why the router fails: task-type-level signal.}
5	The router diagnostic (\S\ref{sec:router-diagnostic}) reveals a structural mismatch: death avoidance is a task-type-level binary property (10/11 types carry death risk), but step-level features correlate weakly because death risk depends on multi-step trajectories. The 4B router improves at episode level ($59.0\%$) without step-level gains ($56$--$59\%$), confirming task-type classification; cross-task-type transfer yields chance accuracy (49.2\%; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}).
6	
7	\paragraph{Non-triviality of death dominance.}
8	While death dominance follows from the penalty structure (\S\ref{sec:daf}), two consequences were not predictable a priori: $10{\times}$ scaling to 72B fails to eliminate it (DAF${}= 92.4\%$), and Reflexion self-correction yields DAF${}>100\%$ (death avoidance \emph{more} than accounts for the full gap). All four interventions leave ${\geq}12$~pp residual gaps (Appendix~\ref{sec:threshold-routing}); cross-model DR stability ($36$--$43\%$) confirms death vulnerability is a task-environment property. All 105 deaths trace to systematic misuse of the answer-submission action (Appendices~\ref{app:failure-modes}--\ref{app:grounding-ablation}). The 7 excluded agent-floor tasks---where both strategies achieve near-zero scores---represent a symmetric failure regime in which death asymmetry is absent. Our framework correctly diagnoses these as ``no signal'' episodes, a design expectation rather than a counterexample to death dominance.
9	
10	\paragraph{Practical implications.}
11	DAF ranges from $7.4\%$ ($P{=}0$) to $90.1\%$ ($P{=}100$; Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}); the penalty-invariant label fraction (78.9\%) is the primary diagnostic. Death dominance diagnosis is actionable: a ${\sim}$50-episode pilot classifies the environment before committing to router development (Appendix~\ref{app:decision-framework}). When $f_{\text{death}} > 75\%$, deterministic routing matches or exceeds learned alternatives; since routing is relevant in only 38.8\% of episodes (\S\ref{sec:death-decomp}), even optimal routing yields modest gains: the oracle margin is 2.89 points (\S\ref{sec:baselines}), bounding practical routing value in death-dominated environments.
12	
13	
14	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_015YDmYgSn5NPpMiaYE1p3Gk`</summary>

```text
56	\end{tabular}
57	\caption{Internal-strategy death rates under agent improvement methods. Top: all 11 tasks (72B: independently collected at both internal and deterministic). Bottom: 10 death-prone tasks (\texttt{find-non-living-thing} excluded, 0\% death at all scales). All interventions reduce death rate but none approaches deterministic-level safety.}
58	\label{tab:agent-improvement}
59	\end{table}
60	
61	We test whether agent-level improvement reduces death dominance (Table~\ref{tab:agent-improvement}). Retry ($N{=}5$ attempts without self-correction) yields marginal reduction on death-prone tasks ($43.5\%$ vs.\ $48.0\%$ baseline, $\Delta = -4.5$~pp). Reflexion~\citep{reflexion-2023} (verbal self-reflection between trials) reduces death to 31.4\% across all 11 tasks by rescuing 29.3\% of first-trial deaths, but the gap to deterministic remains 15.1~pp; on the 10 death-prone tasks, Reflexion achieves ${\sim}$35\%. Under Reflexion ($N{=}3$, matched-episode analysis), $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = -0.47$, yielding DAF $> 100\%$: the deterministic advantage is entirely attributable to death avoidance, and in non-death episodes the self-correcting agent slightly outperforms the deterministic baseline. Scaling to Qwen2.5-72B ($10{\times}$ parameters; both internal and deterministic strategies independently collected at 72B, 255 episodes across all 11 task types) reduces death rate to $30.6\%$. The 72B deterministic strategy achieves $2.75\%$ death rate ($\Delta\text{DR} = +27.8$~pp), and $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = +2.28$, yielding DAF $= 92.4\%$ (7B: $90.4\%$). This $10{\times}$ scaling analysis (distinct from the 7--9B cross-family replication in \S\ref{sec:cross-model}) shows that while 72B reduces the internal death rate from $42.9\%$ to $30.6\%$, DAF \emph{increases} because the deterministic strategy also becomes safer ($2.75\%$ vs.\ $16.3\%$), making the proportional dominance of death avoidance more extreme.
62	
63	\paragraph{Dose-response analysis.}
64	We extend both interventions to $N{=}5$ trials on death-prone tasks ($n{=}200$; Figure~\ref{fig:dose-response}). Retry is nearly flat ($48.0\% \to 43.5\%$, $\Delta = -4.5$~pp over five trials). Reflexion drops steeply to $34.0\%$ at $N{=}3$ ($\Delta = -13.5$~pp, $z = 2.75$, $p = 0.003$) then stabilizes ($34$--$36.5\%$ for $N \geq 3$, pairwise $p > 0.5$). With $n{=}200$ paired episodes, the minimum detectable effect is ${\sim}8$~pp ($\alpha = 0.05$, 80\% power): the key $N{=}1{\to}3$ decline is well-powered; the stabilization claim ($N{=}3{\to}5$, $+1.0$~pp) is also supported, since a ${\geq}8$~pp improvement would be detected. We interpret the plateau as suggestive of a Reflexion ceiling (per-task breakdown in Appendix~\ref{app:dose-response}).
65	
```

```text
1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM agent routing (deciding when to rely on internal reasoning versus external tool use) is typically addressed by training routers directly, without first understanding what mechanistic signal drives the routing preference. This absence of diagnostic methods risks building routers atop confounded or trivial signal. We propose an episode-level counterfactual framework that compares pure-strategy outcomes (internal-only vs.\ deterministic-only) to decompose routing signal into its mechanistic components before any router is constructed. Applying this framework to ScienceWorld (11 task types, 245 episodes), we find that 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels arise from death asymmetry: the internal strategy triggers catastrophic failure while the deterministic strategy survives. This label fraction is penalty-independent. A derived score-weighted measure, the Death Avoidance Factor, reaches 88--90\% across three 7--9B model families (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), confirming convergence on a single dominant mechanism. Cross-environment probes on MATH and ALFWorld diagnose qualitatively different routing mechanisms (no extractable signal and information asymmetry, respectively), illustrating the framework's applicability. A ${\sim}$50-episode diagnostic pilot provides preliminary signal for identifying the dominant routing mechanism given a large effect size ($d = 3.02$); environments with smaller effects may require larger samples, enabling informed build-vs-buy decisions before expensive router training.
3	\end{abstract}
4

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01ADDS82mSrGthraM3JPzWba`</summary>

```text
37	\section*{Limitations}
38	
39	\paragraph{Environmental scope.}
40	Our primary analysis uses a single environment (ScienceWorld, 11 of 30 task types) with one backbone (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), though extended validation of representative excluded tasks confirms the pattern (Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). The $-100$ death penalty amplifies DAF; environments with softer penalties would exhibit lower DAF values (Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}). The 19 excluded task types (63\%) lack routing signal because both strategies typically time out within 30 steps; increasing the step budget could surface non-death-dominated signal that our current setup does not capture. Cross-environment analyses on MATH and ALFWorld (\S\ref{sec:cross-env}, \S\ref{sec:discussion}) provide preliminary supporting evidence but do not conclusively establish generalization. A Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct cross-model replication (11 task types, $N{=}220$; \S\ref{sec:cross-model}) shows death dominance replicates with direction consistency 10/10 while vulnerability magnitude varies ($+30.0$~pp vs.\ Qwen $+26.5$~pp), though both models are in the 7--8B parameter range; Qwen2.5-72B ($10{\times}$ scale) reduces death rate but does not eliminate death dominance (\S\ref{sec:agent-improvement}); validation beyond 72B remains future work. Our quantitative results (DAF $= 90.4\%$, label fractions) are specific to ScienceWorld; the framework's generality claim is methodological (the diagnostic procedure applies to any environment with asymmetric fatal outcomes), not empirical (the specific percentages may differ across environments).
41	
42	\paragraph{Methodological constraints.}
43	Our internal-reasoning baseline uses single-pass ReAct~\citep{react-2023}. Reflexion~\citep{reflexion-2023} reduces death rate from 42.9\% to 31.4\% (\S\ref{sec:agent-improvement}), but more aggressive self-correction or Tree-of-Thought backtracking~\citep{tot-2023} may further reduce internal death rates. Episode-level labels assign a single routing preference to entire episodes, which may be suboptimal for individual steps (particularly those preceding death events). However, of 2{,}040 training steps only 0.3\% exhibit strict directional conflict (step-level oracle opposite to episode label); the remaining apparent mislabeling consists of episode labels applied to steps where neither strategy has a step-level advantage (60.3\% pre-event neutral). The issue is granularity mismatch (applying correct-direction labels to uninformative steps), not directional error. Training on pure-strategy trajectories also introduces distribution shift at deployment~\citep{dagger-2011}. Under mixed-strategy routing with oracle death prediction, switching to deterministic at the step before death could reduce the internal death rate; even under optimistic assumptions (perfect step-level death prediction), a residual gap of ${>}10$~pp between strategies is expected, as fatal trajectories involve cumulative hallucination errors preceding the death event. More broadly, projecting episode-level labels onto 10--50 sequential steps with path-dependent state introduces compounding error: early routing decisions alter the state trajectory, potentially invalidating later labels derived from the episode outcome.
44	
45	\paragraph{Statistical limitations.}
46	Episodes within the same task type are not fully independent because they share environment dynamics, though they use different random seeds. Cluster bootstrap with $K{=}11$ task-type clusters yields wider CIs ($[73.3\%, 100.0\%]$); with $K < 40$ clusters, these should be interpreted cautiously~\citep{cameron-2008,webb-2023}. A wild cluster bootstrap ($K{=}11$, $p < 0.001$; Appendix~\ref{app:wild-cluster}) and 100\% directional consistency across all 28 death-prone tasks (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task-ci}) mitigate this concern.
47	
48	\paragraph{Pure-strategy upper bound.}
49	Our counterfactual analysis compares pure strategies (always-internal vs.\ always-deterministic). In mixed routing, the router can selectively invoke deterministic mode on high-risk steps, potentially reducing death asymmetry. Thus, DAF${=}90.4\%$ represents an upper bound on death avoidance's contribution to optimized routing; the actual contribution under mixed strategies may be lower.
50	
51	\paragraph{Diagnostic scope.}
52	Our framework is diagnostic rather than prescriptive: it identifies what drives routing decisions but does not optimize the routing policy itself. Extending the counterfactual decomposition to prescribe optimal mixed-strategy routing (for example, by combining DAF analysis with online policy learning) is a natural next step.
53	
54	\section*{Ethics Statement}
55	
56	This work uses ScienceWorld \citep{scienceworld-2022}, a publicly available text-based simulation environment, and does not involve human subjects or private data. All evaluation environments (ScienceWorld, ALFWorld, MATH) are released under permissive open-source licenses (Apache 2.0 / MIT). All models used (Qwen2.5 family, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B-it) are publicly available for research use. All language models are accessed via standard APIs or publicly released weights. Experiments were conducted on 2$\times$ NVIDIA RTX 5090 (32GB) and 4$\times$ NVIDIA RTX 6000 PRO (96GB), totaling approximately 200 GPU-hours, comprising ${\sim}$140 hours for episode collection (counterfactual inference across three 7--9B model families plus Qwen2.5-72B backbone scaling, Reflexion and retry baselines, and cross-environment validation on ALFWorld and MATH), ${\sim}$35 hours for router training (LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B across 4--8 seeds per scale, including augmentation and ablation variants), and ${\sim}$25 hours for evaluation and analysis. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.
57	
58	\bibliography{references}
59	
60	\appendix
61	
62	\noindent\textbf{Appendix Overview.}
63	\hyperref[app:sci]{A.~Signal Concentration Index} $\cdot$
64	\hyperref[app:labeling]{B.~Labeling Quality Audit} $\cdot$
65	\hyperref[app:per-task]{C.~Per-Task Decomposition} $\cdot$
66	\hyperref[app:correlation]{D.~Correlation Robustness} $\cdot$

The $-100$ death penalty amplifies DAF; environments with softer penalties would exhibit lower DAF values (Appendix~\\ref{app:penalty}). The 19 excluded task types (63\\%) lack routing signal because both strategies typically time out within 30 steps; increasing the step budget could surface non-death-dominated signal that our current setup does not capture. Cross-environment analyses on MATH and ALFWorld (\\S\\ref{sec:cross-env}, \\S\\ref{sec:discussion}) provide preliminary supporting evidence but do not conclusively establish generalization. A Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct cross-model replication (11 task types, $N{=}220$; \\S\\ref{sec:cross-model}) shows death dominance replicates with direction consistency 10/10 while vulnerability magnitude varies ($+30.0$~pp vs.\\ Qwen $+26.5$~pp), though both models are in the 7--8B parameter range; Qwen2.5-72B ($10{\\times}$ scale) reduces death rate but does not eliminate death dominance (\\S\\ref{sec:agent-improvement}); validation beyond 72B remains future work. Our quantitative results (DAF $= 90.4\\%$, label fractions) are specific to ScienceWorld; the framework's generality claim is methodological (the diagnostic procedure applies to any environment with asymmetric fatal outcomes), not empirical (the specific percentages may differ across environments).\n\n\\paragraph{Methodological constraints.}\nOur internal-reasoning baseline uses single-pass ReAct~\\citep{react-2023}. Reflexion~\\citep{reflexion-2023} reduces death rate from 42.9\\% to 31.4\\% (\\S\\ref{sec:agent-improvement}), but more aggressive self-correction or Tree-of-Thought backtracking~\\citep{tot-2023} may further reduce internal death rates. Episode-level labels assign a single routing preference to entire episodes, which may be suboptimal for individual steps (particularly those preceding death events). However, of 2{,}040 training steps only 0.3\\% exhibit strict directional conflict (step-level oracle opposite to episode label); the remaining apparent mislabeling consists of episode labels applied to steps where neither strategy has a step-level advantage (60.3\\% pre-event neutral). The issue is granularity mismatch (applying correct-direction labels to uninformative steps), not directional error. Training on pure-strategy trajectories also introduces distribution shift at deployment~\\citep{dagger-2011}. Under mixed-strategy routing with oracle death prediction, switching to deterministic at the step before death could reduce the internal death rate; even under optimistic assumptions (perfect step-level death prediction), a residual gap of ${>}10$~pp between strategies is expected, as fatal trajectories involve cumulative hallucination errors preceding the death event. More broadly, projecting episode-level labels onto 10--50 sequential steps with path-dependent state introduces compounding error: early routing decisions alter the state trajectory, potentially invalidating later labels derived from the episode outcome.\n\n\\paragraph{Statistical limitations.}\nEpisodes within the same task type are not fully independent because they share environment dynamics, though they use different random seeds. Cluster bootstrap with $K{=}11$ task-type clusters yields wider CIs ($[73.3\\%, 100.0\\%]$); with $K < 40$ clusters, these should be interpreted cautiously~\\citep{cameron-2008,webb-2023}. A wild cluster bootstrap ($K{=}11$, $p < 0.001$; Appendix~\\ref{app:wild-cluster}) and 100\\% directional consistency across all 28 death-prone tasks (Appendix~\\ref{app:per-task-ci}) mitigate this concern.\n\n\\paragraph{Pure-strategy upper bound.}\nOur counterfactual analysis compares pure strategies (always-internal vs.\\ always-deterministic). In mixed routing, the router can selectively invoke deterministic mode on high-risk steps, potentially reducing death asymmetry. Thus, DAF${=}90.4\\%$ represents an upper bound on death avoidance's contribution to optimized routing; the actual contribution under mixed strategies may be lower.\n\n\\paragraph{Diagnostic scope.}\nOur framework is diagnostic rather than prescriptive: it identifies what drives routing decisions but does not optimize the routing policy itself. Extending the counterfactual decomposition to prescribe optimal mixed-strategy routing (for example, by combining DAF analysis with online policy learning) is a natural next step.\n\n\\section*{Ethics Statement}\n\nThis work uses ScienceWorld \\citep{scienceworld-2022}, a publicly available text-based simulation environment, and does not involve human subjects or private data. All evaluation environments (ScienceWorld, ALFWorld, MATH) are released under permissive open-source licenses (Apache 2.0 / MIT). All models used (Qwen2.5 family, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B-it) are publicly available for research use. All language models are accessed via standard APIs or publicly released weights. Experiments were conducted on 2$\\times$ NVIDIA RTX 5090 (32GB) and 4$\\times$ NVIDIA RTX 6000 PRO (96GB), totaling approximately 200 GPU-hours, comprising ${\\sim}$140 hours for episode collection (counterfactual inference across three 7--9B model families plus Qwen2.5-72B backbone scaling, Reflexion and retry baselines, and cross-environment validation on ALFWorld and MATH), ${\\sim}$35 hours for router training (LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B across 4--8 seeds per scale, including augmentation and ablation variants), and ${\\sim}$25 hours for evaluation and analysis. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.\n\n\\bibliography{references}\n\n\\appendix\n\n\\noindent\\textbf{Appendix Overview.}\n\\hyperref[app:sci]{A.~Signal Concentration Index} $\\cdot$\n\\hyperref[app:labeling]{B.~Labeling Quality Audit} $\\cdot$\n\\hyperref[app:per-task]{C.~Per-Task Decomposition} $\\cdot$\n\\hyperref[app:correlation]{D.~Correlation Robustness} $\\cdot$",
      "numLines": 30,

    }

```text
1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Signal granularity and router failure.}
5	The router diagnostic (\S\ref{sec:router-diagnostic}) reveals a structural mismatch: death avoidance is a task-type-level binary property (10/11 types carry death risk), but step-level features correlate weakly because death risk depends on multi-step trajectories. Six router variants (0.6B--4B, classification and generative) converge on near-chance step-level accuracy ($56$--$59\%$); this convergent failure suggests signal limitation, though extreme class imbalance (90.5\% deterministic-preferred, only 9 internal-preferred episodes) means a data-limited explanation remains viable---distinguishing these hypotheses requires ${\sim}$1{,}350 paired episodes to obtain 50 minority-class samples. Cross-task-type transfer yields chance accuracy (49.2\%; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}). Training on pure-strategy labels but deploying under mixed-strategy routing also introduces a distribution shift~\citep{dagger-2011}; the negative result may partly reflect this train--deploy mismatch.
6	
7	\paragraph{Non-triviality and oracle confound.}
8	While death dominance follows from the penalty structure (\S\ref{sec:daf}), two consequences were not predictable a priori: $10{\times}$ scaling to 72B fails to eliminate it (DAF${}= 92.4\%$), and Reflexion yields DAF${}>100\%$. All four interventions leave ${\geq}12$~pp residual gaps (Appendix~\ref{sec:threshold-routing}); cross-model DR stability ($36$--$43\%$) confirms death vulnerability is a task-environment property. An oracle confound arises: is the signal merely ``use the oracle''? Two observations argue otherwise: (a)~$\Delta_{\text{nd}} = 2.84$ (9.6\% of the total gap)---on non-death episodes, internal reasoning provides almost no advantage; (b)~action-space filtering (Appendix~\ref{app:grounding-ablation}) reduces death rate by 19~pp but remains far above deterministic safety (16.3\%).
9	
10	\paragraph{Practical implications and actionability.}
11	The penalty-invariant label fraction (78.9\%) is the primary diagnostic; DAF varies with $P$ (Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}). Since routing is relevant in only 38.8\% of episodes (\S\ref{sec:death-decomp}), even optimal routing yields modest gains (oracle margin 2.89 points; \S\ref{sec:baselines}). When $f_{\text{death}} > 75\%$, deterministic routing matches or exceeds learned alternatives at zero training cost; action-space filtering (Appendix~\ref{app:grounding-ablation}) reduces death rate by 19~pp as a complementary intervention. The diagnostic identifies \emph{when} always-deterministic suffices versus when router development is warranted (as in ALFWorld, where DAF${=}0\%$).
12	
13	
```
